  presses = amount owed / amount taken per press. Measured 29 Aug 2026:
  the upgrade machine takes 20 per press, not 1, so a total on its own
  says nothing. The per-press figure is the deposit object's
  RequiredMoney, followed through the machine's CoinDeposit pointer.
  ARITHMETIC, not observation: it assumes a constant amount per press,
  which is measured for the upgrade machine and assumed elsewhere.

---- DEPOSIT LOOPS: how many presses each one costs ----
  presses = amount owed / amount taken per press. Measured 29 Aug 2026:
  the upgrade machine takes 20 per press, not 1, so a total on its own
  says nothing. The per-press figure is the deposit object's
  RequiredMoney, followed through the machine's CoinDeposit pointer.
  ARITHMETIC, not observation: it assumes a constant amount per press,
  which is measured for the upgrade machine and assumed elsewhere.
